Regarding assessment lessons/units, we don't want any longer to open edx course iframe before having that load on its end the agent embed

The backend now sends back the type ibl_mentor_xblock for an assessment unit children on the course outline 

U might check out the course here http://localhost:3001/platform/babatunde/course-content/course-v1:1718796901+C1+2026-05/agent 

and look out for the course outline endpoint response.

so whenever a particular lesson has a child type ibl_mentor_xblock, consider it as an assessment mentor and directly call the frontend agent-ai element passing the necessary data needed by the agent-ai package instead of calling the edx iframe. Just have the edx iframe usage for the assessment mentor as fallback in case a particular assessment unit doesn't have a child with type ibl_mentor_xblock. can happen if an existing course isn't re-published to send out the new ibl_mentor_xblock type

Course outline endpoint https://api.iblai.org/dm/api/catalog/enrollment/courses/completion/?username=babatunde&course_id=course-v1%3A1718796901%2BC1%2B2026-05&endpoint=outline and sample response for course http://localhost:3001/platform/babatunde/course-content/course-v1:1718796901+C1+2026-05/agent
